A source is where your channels come from
CarTV is just a player and comes with no channels. A source tells it where to find yours:
list.bullet.rectangle
An M3U / M3U8 playlist link, also called an IPTV subscription link
A config file that bundles several playlist links
A Jellyfin or Emby media server you have an account on
Where do I get one?
Your IPTV or streaming provider usually gives you the link 
 check their website, email, or app. Many TV stations and communities also publish public playlists.
